Our contribution is a mechanistic account of why added structure does not improve retrieval over a strong ranker, and identify the conditions under which adding structural interdependence into the retrieval is optimal.

</details>


### [335] [EnvACE: Internalizing Environment Dynamics via World Rehearsal for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06197)
*Zishan Xu,Zhiyuan Yao,Yuxin Chen,Yifu Guo,Zhengxi Lu,Yuquan Lu,Jinyang Huang,Yan Xu,Yasheng Wang,Weinan Zhang,Xingshan Zeng,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnvACE是一种智能体强化学习方法，通过"世界排练"（world rehearsal）替代外部环境交互，让策略交替执行行动和模拟环境响应，在多个基准上超越依赖外部环境的基线，实现强且可迁移的性能，并在测试阶段通过私有排练进一步提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练大语言模型智能体进行长期工具使用通常依赖真实或合成的可执行环境，这些环境的构建和验证成本高昂；外部模拟器又难以与真实世界对齐。因此需要一个不需要外部环境交互的训练方法。

Method: EnvACE采用代理强化学习：策略交替进行行动与排练（world rehearsal）。具体地，策略首先生成工具调用，然后扮演环境角色产生该行动对应的响应，后续决策基于排练得到的响应进行。两个角色通过任务成功奖励进行端到端联合优化。通过世界排练，策略将行动与环境响应之间的关系内化到参数中，形成支持决策的智能体世界模型。

Result: 在BFCL-v4、tau^2-Bench、VitaBench和FinMCP-Bench上，EnvACE取得强且可迁移的性能，在总体评估中超越环境扩展（environment-scaling）基线。受控研究表明世界排练在不同模型规模下持续改善策略学习。在测试阶段，内化的世界模型可在正式执行前进行私有排练，在中等的排练预算下无需额外外部交互即可获得进一步增益。

Conclusion: 世界排练开辟了超越外部环境限制、扩展LLM智能体训练的新路径，表明智能体可以在没有外部环境交互的情况下通过自我模拟环境响应来学习工具使用。

Abstract: Training large language model agents for long-horizon tool use typically relies on interactions with real or synthesized executable environments, whose construction and verification are costly, or on external simulators that are difficult to ground. We introduce EnvACE, an agentic reinforcement learning method that replaces external environment interaction during training with world rehearsal. The policy alternates between acting and rehearsal: it first generates a tool call, then plays the role of the environment to produce the response induced by that action, and conditions subsequent decisions on the rehearsed response. Both roles are jointly optimized end-to-end using task-success rewards. Through world rehearsal, the policy internalizes the relationship between actions and their environment responses in its parameters, yielding an agent world model that directly supports decision making. Across BFCL-v4, tau^2-Bench, VitaBench, and FinMCP-Bench, EnvACE achieves strong and transferable performance, outperforming environment-scaling baselines in the overall evaluation. Controlled studies further show that world rehearsal consistently improves policy learning across model scales. At test time, the internalized world model enables private rehearsal before committed execution, yielding further gains under a moderate rehearsal budget without additional external interaction. Our findings establish world rehearsal as a new path toward scaling LLM agent training beyond the constraints of external environments. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Within-yao/EnvACE.

</details>


### [336] [TS-RAG: Retrieval Augmented Generation for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06223)
*Yixiong Xiao,Congxi Xiao,Jingbo Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TS-RAG框架，将检索增强生成应用于时间序列预测，通过专门设计的reference tokens将输入序列与检索到的相似序列信息有效融合，在多个真实世界预测基准上取得一致的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成(RAG)在大型语言模型中已被证明能通过引入相关外部信息提升性能，但在时间序列预测领域应用有限。虽然直觉上检索相似时间序列作为参考可能提升预测精度，但大多数时间序列模型受限于训练数据不足、参数规模小、缺乏大语言模型的生成能力，简单地将参考序列拼接进输入（如语言模型的做法）可能无效，因此需要专门设计适应时间序列特点的RAG框架。

Method: 提出TS-RAG框架，设计专门的reference tokens用于有效融合输入序列与检索到的相似序列的信息，从而更稳健地捕获复杂的时间动态。该框架在现有时间序列模型的基础上，通过检索机制获取相似序列并利用reference tokens实现信息融合。

Result: 实验结果表明TS-RAG在多个真实世界预测基准上取得了一致的最先进（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: TS-RAG通过专门设计的reference tokens成功将RAG思想迁移到时间序列预测领域，克服了时间序列模型在数据规模、参数规模和大语言模型生成能力方面的限制，有效提升了预测准确率。

Abstract: While deep learning models, particularly transformer-based architectures, have shown impressive performance in time series forecasting, the application of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in this domain remains limited. Since RAG has proven effective in enhancing the capabilities of large language models by incorporating relevant external information, retrieving similar time series sequences as references might also improve accuracy in time series forecasting tasks. However, most time series models are constrained by limited training data, smaller parameter scales, and a lack of the extensive generative capabilities found in large language models. Simply concatenating reference sequences into the prompt, as done in language models, may not yield the expected results. To address these challenges, we propose a novel approach, TS-RAG, which leverages RAG to enhance forecasting performance. The framework introduces specially designed reference tokens to effectively fuse information from the input sequence with that from retrieved similar sequences, enabling a more robust capture of complex temporal dynamics. Experimental results demonstrate that TS-RAG achieves consistent state-of-the-art performance across several real-world forecasting benchmarks.

</details>


### [337] [DASH: Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons for On-Policy Self-Distillation of Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06243)
*ZhiYan Hou,Xinyu Tang,Hongyan An,Jianjin Zhang,Weizhen Wang,Yunyun Han,Gengsheng Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Haiyun Guo,Wenbin Hu,Jinqiao Wang,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons (DASH) 方法，通过将局部蒸馏信号与序列级均值的差距映射为自适应传播门控，控制反向多步聚合，使 token 级别监督权重能够适应生成过程中局部发散度的演变，从而解决标准 OPSD 未能充分利用 rollout 时间结构的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 使用的可验证奖励信号稀疏且位于序列级；OPSD 虽通过教师网络提供密集 token 级监督缓解了稀疏性，但标准 OPSD 对所有局部发散赋予相同系数，忽略了发散值在生成过程中的时序上下文。由于相同幅度的局部散度可能来自不同的分歧历史，仅靠局部标量无法区分这些时序情境，导致无法自适应调整 token 级权重。

Method: DASH 将每个局部蒸馏信号与序列级均值之间的差距映射为自适应传播门控（adaptive propagation gate），再利用这些门控控制反向多步聚合过程，从而根据生成过程中局部发散度的演变动态调整 token 级监督权重。

Result: 在三个数学推理基准和三个模型规模上的实验表明，DASH 在全部基准和全部规模下均优于匹配的 vanilla OPSD 复现结果。由于 DASH 复用 OPSD 已计算的教师和学生分布，其增益不需要额外的教师或学生前向传播。

Conclusion: DASH 通过引入发散度自适应的传播门控，有效利用了 OPSD 训练中 rollouts 的时间结构，在无需额外计算开销的前提下显著提升了数学推理任务的性能，验证了根据局部发散演化动态调整监督权重的重要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models using automatically verifiable outcome signals, but these signals are typically sparse and at the sequence-level. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) mitigates this sparsity by querying a privileged teacher at student-visited prefixes and providing dense token-level distributional supervision. Although this dense supervision alleviates signal sparsity, we find that standard OPSD still underexploits the temporal structure of the rollout. It assigns every local divergence the same coefficient, regardless of its position or the divergence sequence in which it occurs. In on-policy autoregressive generation, the same divergence magnitude can follow different discrepancy histories, reflecting different evolutions of the mismatch between the teacher and student. Since the local scalar alone cannot distinguish these temporal contexts, standard OPSD cannot adapt its token-level weights to the realized discrepancy sequence. To address this limitation, we propose Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons (DASH). DASH maps the gap between each local distillation signal and the sequence-level mean to an adaptive propagation gate and then uses these gates to control backward multi-step aggregation. By doing so, DASH adjusts token-level supervision weights according to how local divergences evolve during generation. Experiments on three mathematical reasoning benchmarks across three model scales show that DASH improves over our matched vanilla OPSD reruns on every benchmark at all three scales. DASH reuses the teacher and student distributions that OPSD already computes, so the gains require no additional teacher or student forward pass.
  Code: https://github.com/DBtxy/DASH-OPSD

</details>


### [338] [Improving the Realism of Synthetic Clinical Benchmarks Under Utility Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06265)
*Omid Bazgir,Md Nasir,Jacob Hoffman,Yang Yang,Manu Agrawal,Anusua Trivedi,Vinay Rao Dandin,Chris Gibbons,Christine Swisher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对医疗企业AI智能体的合成临床基准数据，提出将基准修订形式化为"效用约束下的现实性提升"问题，在保持下游效用检查通过的前提下，通过确定性的数据修订提高基准的结构现实性，并证明内部现实性与对操作数据聚合参考的保真度是两个相关但不同的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成临床基准数据即使通过现有效用检查，仍可能存在结构性不现实的问题，尤其在隐私敏感的医疗场景下操作数据难以获取。现有基准往往只关注效用（utility）检查而忽视数据现实性（realism），导致基于这些基准训练/评估的企业AI智能体可能对真实数据泛化不力。作者希望在不破坏现有实用效用检查的前提下，系统性提升合成基准的现实性。

Method: 将基准修订形式化为效用约束下的现实性优化问题：数据变更需在保持高于操作效用下限的同时提升现实性。作者基于Synthea生成的患者数据构建护理缺口（care-gap）基准，通过演示电子健康记录工作流并经过与操作数据相同的下游管线处理。现实性通过四个方面度量：缺失结构（missingness structure）、简洁性（simplicity）、结构合理性（structural plausibility）和人群对齐（population alignment）。提出两种确定性修订方法，并以朴素稠密化作为对照。

Result: 基线基准极为薄弱：样本对缺失率为79.44%，仅12.75%的行可操作，38.94%的患者无可操作指标，前三令牌集中度达100.0%。两种确定性修订在保持效用下限的同时改善了现实性面板，而朴素稠密化对照则保留了不现实的模板化模式。同时发现内部基准现实性与对操作聚合参考的源保真度是相关但独立的目标。

Conclusion: 合成基准的质量应被明确优化，效用应被视为众多约束之一而非现实性的充分证据。仅依赖效用检查不足以保证合成基准在真实场景中的可靠性，有必要将现实性作为独立优化目标纳入基准设计流程。

Abstract: Synthetic clinical benchmarks for enterprise AI agents can pass existing utility checks and still remain structurally unrealistic, especially in privacy-sensitive healthcare settings where operational data are hard to access. We study how to improve such benchmarks without breaking the downstream utility checks already used in practice.
  We formulate benchmark revision as utility-constrained realism improvement: dataset changes should increase realism while staying above an operational utility floor. We instantiate this idea on a care-gap benchmark derived from Synthea-generated patients exercised through demonstration electronic health record workflows and then processed by the same downstream pipeline as operational data. Realism is measured through missingness structure, simplicity, structural plausibility, and population alignment.
  The baseline benchmark is extremely thin: sampled-pair missingness is 79.44%, only 12.75% of rows are actionable, 38.94% of patients have zero actionable measures, and top-three token concentration reaches 100.0%. Two deterministic revisions improve these panels while remaining above the current utility floor, whereas a naive densification control preserves unrealistic templating. We further show that internal benchmark realism and source fidelity to an aggregate operational reference are related but distinct objectives. These results suggest that synthetic benchmark quality should be optimized explicitly, with utility treated as one constraint rather than as sufficient evidence of realism.

</details>


### [339] [The Illusion of Visual Tool-Use: A Causal Audit of Thinking with Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06270)
*Zhiheng Wang,Bo Peng,Lai Wei,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个因果审计框架（CauAudit），用于评估多模态大模型（MLLM）中"思维-图像"视觉工具使用的真实因果效应。通过将视觉工具使用建模为因果图，并在策略、轨迹和步骤三个层面进行干预，作者发现工具使用虽然带来了聚合准确率提升，但实际因果有效推理仅在少数校准良好的轨迹中发生，揭示了一种"视错觉工具使用"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"思维-图像"范式通过crop-and-zoom等主动视觉操作增强多模态LLM，但这类操作往往只带来边际甚至负面的性能提升，同时产生更高的token开销。模型还可能反复裁剪无关区域，在直接推理能正确回答的问题上失败。作者质疑：返回的视觉证据是否真正因果性地影响了答案？这一问题在现有文献中尚未得到系统回答。

Method: 作者将视觉工具使用形式化为因果图，区分观察介导路径和行动诱导捷径。在三个层面实施干预审计：(1) 策略层面：比较工具使用与直接推理；(2) 轨迹层面：在rollout过程中破坏所有观察；(3) 步骤层面：在固定前缀下反事实替换单个观察。其中步骤层面的估计量——Visual Evidence Gain——用于隔离每个返回观察的因果贡献。实验覆盖六个代表性模型和五个细粒度感知基准。

Result: 发现了策略校准不良的两种失败模式：一是"不看就调用"（Calling Without Looking），即返回的观察对答案没有因果效应；二是"看而不规划"（Looking Without Planning），即观察有信息但调用调度不连贯。轨迹层面的诊断将策略层面的准确率增益分解，发现增益集中在少数"校准良好"的子集中，揭示了视觉工具使用的"视错觉"现象。

Conclusion: 尽管聚合准确率有提升，视觉工具使用在广泛的rollout范围内并非因果有效。这一发现要求研究社区重新审视视觉工具使用的评估方法，仅依赖最终准确率可能掩盖工具调用策略的深层缺陷。

Abstract: The "thinking-with-images" paradigm equips multimodal LLMs with active visual operations such as crop-and-zoom. However, models using these operations often achieve only marginal or negative gains over direct inference at substantially higher token cost. They may also repeatedly crop irrelevant regions and fail on questions that direct inference answers correctly. We ask whether the returned visual evidence causally affects the answer. To answer this question, we formulate visual tool-use as a causal graph that separates observation-mediated paths from action-induced shortcuts. We then audit it through interventions at the three levels: policy (comparing tool-use with direct inference), trajectory (corrupting all observations during rollout), and step (counterfactually replacing one individual observation under a fixed prefix). Our step-level estimand, Visual Evidence Gain, isolates the contribution of each returned observation. Across six representative models and five fine-grained perception benchmarks, we uncover policy miscalibration with two failure modes. In Calling Without Looking, returned observations have no causal effect on the answer. In Looking Without Planning, observations are informative but the call schedule is incoherent. A trajectory-level diagnostic decomposes the policy-level accuracy gain and shows that the gain is concentrated in a Calibrated minority. We term this discrepancy the illusion of visual tool-use: despite aggregate accuracy gains, visual tool-use is not causally effective across a broad range of rollouts. The code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/CauAudit.

</details>


### [340] [QuanTiMedAI: Quantum-Enhanced Time-Series Model guided by Agentic AI for Cardiac Arrest Mortality Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06294)
*Mutasim Fuad Sarker,Adiba Rahman Namira,Wafa Binte Alam,Md Adnan Arefeen,Mahzabeen Emu,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QuanTiMedAI框架，将代理型大语言模型（LLM）用于临床特征发现，与紧凑量子循环网络相结合，用于ICU心脏骤停患者的死亡率预测。在MIMIC-IV队列上，该框架以仅605个参数达到AUROC 0.852，超越当前最先进基线约2.9%，并系统验证了各架构设计选择的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏骤停在ICU中致死率极高，但现有死亡率预测研究多依赖入院早期的静态摘要，忽视了患者在ICU住院期间随时间推移的生理恶化与恢复过程。这种时序动态信息的缺失限制了预测性能。

Method: QuanTiMedAI结合两大部分：(1) 代理型LLM进行临床信息特征发现与选择，替代传统特征选择方法；(2) 紧凑型量子循环网络进行时序感知的死亡率预测，通过非线性特征增强在极低参数量下实现高性能。在MIMIC-IV心脏骤停患者队列上进行实验，并进行结构化消融研究验证各组件贡献。

Result: 代理型LLM引导的特征选择持续优于传统特征选择方法；量子架构以605个参数达到AUROC 0.852，较现有最先进基线提升约2.9%。量子增强的序列建模在参数量显著更少的情况下超越经典循环网络。

Conclusion: 研究证明量子增强序列建模能够以极少的参数量超越经典循环网络，代理型AI与量子计算的结合为ICU死亡率预测提供了新范式，同时通过消融研究系统验证了每个架构设计选择的有效性。

Abstract: Cardiac arrest remains one of the most lethal conditions encountered in intensive care units. Despite the growing availability of electronic health record data, existing mortality prediction studies in this population largely depend on static summaries derived from early admission. Such approaches ignore the temporal progression of physiological deterioration and recovery that unfolds throughout a patient's ICU stay. To address this limitation, we introduce QuanTiMedAI, a quantum-agentic framework developed for cardiac arrest mortality prediction using agentic AI guided quantum enhancement time series model. The proposed system combines an agentic large language model (LLM) for clinically informed feature discovery with a compact quantum recurrent network for temporality aware mortality prediction. Our findings demonstrate that agentic LLM-guided feature selection consistently outperforms conventional feature selection approaches, and the proposed quantum architecture achieves competitive predictive performance through nonlinear feature enhancement while keeping the number of parameters very low. Through extensive experimentation on a MIMIC-IV cohort of cardiac arrest patients, QuanTiMedAI's quantum-enhanced architecture attains an AUROC of 0.852 using only 605 parameters, an improvement of approximately 2.9\% over a current state-of-the-art baseline for this task. A structured ablation study systematically validates the contribution of each architectural design choice. These results show that quantum-enhanced sequential modeling can exceed classical recurrent networks while using substantially fewer parameters.

</details>


### [341] [Bias Analysis of L2 Speaking Assessment Systems Using Concept Activation Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06300)
*Arya Labroo,Mengjie Qian,Kate Knill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了基于概念激活向量（CAV）的偏见检测方法至两个神经口语评估系统（基于BERT的文本评分器和基于Whisper的多模态评分器），并研究稀疏自编码器（SAE）是否能在稀疏潜在空间中提供更清晰的概念方向。研究发现概念的可恢复性和灵敏度都高度依赖于模型架构和表示方式，SAE提升了概念的线性可恢复性但削弱了原始激活空间的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动口语评估系统在高风险场景中日益普及，其评分必须依赖口语能力而非无关的说话者属性（如母语或年龄）。然而，基于Transformer的基础模型虽然提高了评分准确性，但其黑盒表征使得公平性和可解释性分析变得困难。此前基于CAV的工作仅针对基于特征的评分器，本文旨在将CAV分析扩展至更复杂的神经口语评估系统。

Method: 本文在基于文本的BERT评分器和基于Whisper的语音-文本多模态评分器上应用CAV分析。CAV将人类可解释的概念表示为模型激活空间中的方向，通过梯度灵敏度指标区分"概念是否被编码在内部表征中"与"概念是否影响预测分数"。由于CAV依赖线性可分性，而在复杂神经嵌入空间中这种性质较难满足，作者还研究在稀疏潜在空间中学习CAV并映射回激活空间的SAE方法。

Result: 分析表明，概念的可恢复性主要取决于被探测的表示和架构，而非概念本身；对概念的灵敏度同样具有架构依赖性。SAE使得概念在稀疏空间中更易于线性恢复，但会削弱原始激活空间的灵敏度，尤其是在低维层中。

Conclusion: 在审计口语评估系统的偏见时，需要区分概念的可恢复性与概念对评分的影响（灵敏度），二者是不同维度的度量，不能混为一谈。这一发现为构建公平且可解释的自动化口语评估系统提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Automatic speaking assessment systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings to mark second language (L2) learners' speaking tests, making it critical to show that their scores depend on speaking proficiency rather than irrelevant speaker attributes such as first language (L1) or age. Transformer-based foundation models have improved the accuracy of these L2 speaking graders, but their black-box representations make fairness and interpretability analysis more difficult. Building on prior work that used Concept Activation Vectors (CAVs) to detect bias towards unwanted attributes (`concepts') in feature-based graders, we extend CAV-based analysis to two neural speaking assessment systems: a text-based BERT grader and a speech-and-text multimodal grader based on Whisper. CAVs represent human-interpretable concepts as directions in a model's activation space, allowing us to distinguish between whether a concept is encoded in a model's internal representations and whether it influences the predicted score, the latter quantified using a gradient-based sensitivity metric. Since CAVs rely on linear separability, which is less likely in complex neural embedding spaces, we also investigate whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide cleaner concept directions by learning CAVs in a sparse latent space and mapping them back to activation space. Our analysis shows that concept recoverability depends strongly on the representation and architecture being probed, rather than on the concept alone. Sensitivity to concepts is also architecture-dependent. SAEs make concepts more linearly recoverable, but attenuate the original activation-space sensitivity, especially in low-dimensional layers. These findings highlight the need to distinguish concept recoverability from concept influence when auditing bias in speaking assessment systems.

</details>


### [342] [TRAJDEBUG: Tracing Error Lifecycle to Identify Critical Failures in Long-Horizon Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06346)
*Yunjia Qi,Zehua Yin,Xintong Shi,Hao Peng,Songyuanyi Lu,Yixian Liu,Richeng Xuan,Yuhong Liu,Zhichao Hu,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Bin Xu,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajDebug，一个基于错误生命周期追踪的框架，用于在LLM智能体系统的长失败轨迹中定位关键错误步骤，并构建了包含486条人工标注失败轨迹的基准TrajErrBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体系统在复杂领域表现出色，但存在级联错误和调试困难的问题。关键错误检测旨在定位失败轨迹中导致最终失败的早期错误步骤，但面临两大挑战：一是长轨迹中证据分散，难以识别单个错误；二是失败轨迹常包含多个局部错误，只有部分对最终失败负责。

Method: TrajDebug采用错误生命周期追踪框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 多粒度历史压缩和基于证据的错误识别，以应对长轨迹中错误发现问题；(2) 通过追踪每个错误的解析状态和终端影响来实现关键归因。同时构建TrajErrBench基准，包含486条来自Tau2Bench和SWE-Bench Pro的人工标注失败轨迹，覆盖工具使用和编码场景。

Result: 在多个智能体基准上的实验表明，TrajDebug在整体性能上优于现有基线；应用研究进一步证明其诊断结果能提供可操作的反馈，提升下游智能体的成功率。

Conclusion: TrajDebug有效解决了长轨迹中关键错误检测的挑战，其诊断反馈可提升下游智能体的性能。代码和数据将公开发布以促进后续研究。

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems have shown remarkable capabilities in complex domains, while suffering from cascading errors and difficulty in debugging. Critical error detection aims to locate the earliest error step in a failed trajectory that is responsible for the final failure. However, progress faces two main challenges. First, long trajectories make it difficult to identify individual errors, since the evidence for judging a step may be scattered across distant instructions, observations, and prior context. Second, failed trajectories often contain multiple local errors with different downstream effects, only some of which remain responsible for the final failure. In this work, we propose TrajDebug, an error-lifecycle tracing framework that addresses long-trajectory error discovery with multi-granularity history compression and evidence-based error identification, and supports critical attribution by tracing each error's resolution status and terminal impact. We further construct TrajErrBench, a benchmark of 486 manually annotated failed trajectories from Tau2Bench and SWE-Bench Pro, covering realistic tool-use and coding scenarios. Experiments across diverse agent benchmarks show that TrajDebug achieves the best overall performance over existing baselines, and application studies further demonstrate that its diagnoses provide actionable feedback for improving downstream agent success. We will release the codes and data to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [343] [Challenges in Evaluating Explanation Methods for Static and Evolving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06351)
*Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了可解释人工智能（XAI）评估不足的局限性，通过DetoxAI图像识别系统进行说明，并提出了用于图像分类解释的人类基础评估方法，以及适应概念漂移数据流的解释适配方法（包括反事实解释），最后讨论了数据、模型与解释共同演化的追踪挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决XAI领域中解释方法评估不足的问题，特别是在实际应用（如空间场景）中，解释方法需要面对动态变化的数据流和概念漂移，而现有评估体系和方法难以适应这些挑战。

Method: 以DetoxAI图像识别系统（面向偏见检测和概念遗忘）为例说明XAI评估的不足；提出人类基础的图像分类解释评估方法；探索适应概念漂移数据流的解释方法，重点讨论了反事实解释的适配经验；并讨论了数据、模型和解释协同演化的追踪方法。

Result: 展示了XAI评估不足的具体案例，给出了人类基础评估的示例，积累了反事实解释适配概念漂移问题的实践经验，并揭示了数据-模型-解释共同演化的追踪挑战。

Conclusion: XAI评估需要更加全面的人类基础评估，解释方法需要具备适应动态数据环境的鲁棒性，同时需要新的方法论来追踪数据、模型和解释之间的协同演化关系。

Abstract: This paper addresses the limitations of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) with respect to insufficient evaluation. They are illustrated through the DetoxAI image recognition system for bias detection and concept unlearning. Then, an example of a human-grounded evaluation of methods for explaining image classification is presented. The paper further explores methods for adapting explanations to evolving data streams with concept drift. Experiences with adapting counterfactuals for this problem are discussed. Finally it is related to the challenges of tracking the co-evolution of data, models, and explanations.\footnote{This paper has been accepted for a publication in J.Nalepa (ed) Explainable AI in Space. Proceedings of EASi 2026 Workshop at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 Bremen, Springer CCIS vol 3107 (2016).}

</details>


### [344] [The Low Frequency Trap: Video Language Models Fail at Simple Event Bookkeeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06361)
*Sarvesh Baskar,Zikui Cai,Shayan Shabihi,Anirudh Satheesh,Muhammad R. Islam,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于可执行事件轨迹（trace）的"参数化剖析"方法，在三个受控视频任务（弹球壁接触、视觉眨眼、类别状态转换）共2,190个视频中，系统改变事件数量N和频率F来评估事件计数能力。结果显示模型表现出阶段性的时间失败模式：对持久状态转换在80%可靠阈值下最多能数到12个事件，但对瞬态眨眼事件没有可靠的正计数区域；高数量高频率区域仅0.2%答案正确，仅恢复18.1%真实事件。提高采样率虽能将弹球准确率从19.6%提升到29.3%，但报告序列与真值吻合率仅3.7%，说明额外帧可能虚增最终分数但不产生真实事件恢复。轨迹级剖析将视频评估从聚合准确率转向细粒度的时间推理失败诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实世界视频基准虽然覆盖面广，但固定片段将事件数量、速率、持续时间和视觉复杂度纠缠在一起，难以隔离失败模式。已有的程序化基准虽提供更好的控制，但只给最终答案打分，无法对照可执行真值审计报告的事件。这促使作者引入轨迹级参数化剖析方法，以精确锁定时间推理失败的具体原因。

Method: 作者设计了三个受控视频任务（弹球壁接触、视觉眨眼、类别状态转换），在每个视频中变化事件数量N和事件频率F，同时保持渲染固定。每个视频附带可执行事件轨迹（executable event trace），用于能力曲面估计和时间戳级别评估。此外还通过提高采样率和改变提示策略来测试视觉访问是否是主要瓶颈。

Result: 在80%可靠性阈值下，Gemini 3.6 Flash在0.5和1.0Hz下可靠计数持久状态转换最多12个事件，但对瞬态眨眼事件没有任何可靠正计数区域。高数量高频率区域最终答案正确率仅0.2%，事件恢复率仅18.1%。提高采样率将弹球准确率从19.6%提升到29.3%，但序列一致性仅3.7%。不同提示策略增益有限，真实视频评估也显示成功集中在低事件数量。

Conclusion: 事件表征方式决定模型最初能否访问证据，这一限制会随着数量和频率的增加而加剧。提高采样率可以虚增最终分数但不产生忠实的事件恢复。轨迹级剖析方法将视频评估从聚合准确率指标转向对时间推理失败点的详细诊断，为理解大模型视频认知能力的边界提供了新范式。

Abstract: Real-world video benchmarks provide broad coverage, but their fixed clips entangle event count, rate, duration, and visual complexity, making failure modes hard to isolate. While existing programmatic benchmarks offer better control, they score only the final answer rather than auditing reported events against executable ground truth. To bridge this gap, we introduce trace-grounded parametric profiling for event counting in three controlled video tasks: bouncing-ball wall contacts, visual blinks, and categorical state transitions. Across 2,190 videos, we vary event count N and frequency F while holding rendering fixed. Each video includes an executable event trace for capability-surface estimation and timestamp-level evaluation. Our results reveal a staged temporal failure. At an 80% reliability threshold, Gemini 3.6 Flash reliably counts persistent state transitions up to 12 events at 0.5 and 1.0 Hz, yet demonstrates no reliable positive-count region for transient blinking events. Thus, event representation dictates whether a model initially accesses evidence -- a limitation that compounds as count and frequency increase. In the high-count, high-frequency regime, only 0.2% of final counts are correct and the model recovers just 18.1% of true events. To test if visual access is the primary bottleneck, we increase sampling rate. Although this boosts Bounce Ball accuracy from 19.6% to 29.3%, the reported sequence agrees with ground truth only 3.7% of the time. Extra frames can therefore inflate final scores without producing faithful event recovery. Different prompting strategies yield similarly limited gains, and real-world video evaluations show the same concentration of success at low event counts. Ultimately, trace-grounded profiling shifts video evaluation from aggregate accuracy metrics to a detailed diagnostic of where temporal reasoning fails.

</details>


### [345] [Tracing the Heart: An Evidence-Linked Pipeline for Heart-Failure Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06366)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Michelle Hu,Dorisa Shehi,Daniel Kang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Koustuv Saha,Christian Poellabauer,Christopher Lee,Sajeev Singh,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Zeeshan Ahmed,Priyadarshini Kachroo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Nimblemind多智能体系统（nMAS），一种基于证据链和规则评分框架的自动化心衰电子病历特征工程流水线，在500条虚拟患者记录上生成132个结构化特征和70个评分聚合特征，将HFrEF分型的AUROC从0.895提升至0.963，HFpEF从0.870提升至0.910，并通过独立LLM审核获得81.5%的评分，验证了自动化、可审计特征工程的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子病历特征工程是临床研究和AI中的主要瓶颈，占数据科学家39-45%的工作量。在心衰领域尤其突出，因为需要整合碎片化的EHR数据与疾病特异性、基于指南的临床推理。现有的基于规则和基于大语言模型的方法只能提供部分自动化，缺乏可维护性和证据可追溯性。

Method: 开发了Nimblemind多智能体系统（nMAS），这是一个基于证据链接和评分规则框架的自动化心衰特征工程流水线。系统在来自9个EHR源表的500条虚拟患者记录上进行评估，生成132个结构化特征和70个评分聚合特征，并通过受限LLM进行结构完整性、规则合规性、来源可追溯性验证和审计。

Result: 添加聚合特征后，HFrEF表型分型的留出集AUROC从0.895提高到0.963，HFpEF从0.870提高到0.910。独立的基于LLM的评分评估在证据支持和方法学合理性方面为特征评分为最高分的81.5%。

Conclusion: 研究证明了自动化、可审计特征工程在复杂心血管EHR数据上的可行性，但评估仅限于单机构队列，需要外部验证。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) feature engineering is a major bottleneck in clinical research and AI, accounting for 39-45% of data scientists' workload. This is especially pronounced in heart failure, which affects an estimated 6.7 million U.S. adults and requires integrating fragmented EHR data with disease-specific, guideline-based clinical reasoning. Existing rule-based and large language model (LLM)-based approaches offer only partial automation with limited maintainability and evidence traceability. We developed the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), an evidence-linked, rubric-grounded pipeline for automated heart-failure feature engineering, and evaluated it on 500 dummy patient records from nine EHR source tables. nMAS generated 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features, verified for structural integrity, rubric compliance, and provenance, and audited by a restricted LLM. Adding the aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF phenotyping, and an independent LLM-based rubric assessment of evidence support and methodological soundness scored the features at 81.5% of maximum points. These results demonstrate the feasibility of automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data, though evaluation was limited to a single-institution cohort and external validation is needed.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [346] [Computationally Efficient Collaborative Communication Via Regularity-Based Coarsening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05327)
*Mark Bedaywi,Scott Emmons,Nika Haghtalab,Stuart Russell*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体信息聚合中的通信协议设计问题，证明了只要存在短的高效用协议，就能高效地进行通信。具体地，给出了一个多项式时间算法，仅用2^{O(CC_α(G))}/ε²比特通信即可达到近似最优效用，且证明该指数依赖是紧的（除非P=NP）。技术上，提出了Frieze-Kannan弱正则引理的新变体，构造了一个"粗化"定理作为核心工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体信息聚合中，如何设计高效通信协议以达成目标效用是一个核心问题。此前的工作依赖于结构化假设（如信息替代性或弱可学习性），但这些假设过于严格。本文旨在弱化这些假设，证明只要存在短的高效用协议（以CC_α(G)度量），就足以实现高效通信，填补了现有文献中一直悬而未决的空白。

Method: 本文提出两个主要技术贡献：(1) 一个多项式时间算法，利用Frieze-Kannan弱正则引理的新颖强化版本，构造一个粗化游戏Ĝ，将智能体的观测空间划分为常数大小的分区，使得G和Ĝ对任意短通信协议不可区分；以此为基础，算法仅用2^{O(CC_α(G))}/ε²比特通信即可达到效用α-ε。(2) 通过归约证明，除非P=NP，任何多项式时间算法都至少需要2^{CC_α(G)-2}比特，从而证明指数依赖的紧性。

Result: (1) 对任意可实现的目标效用α，给出了poly(n,m,1/ε)运行时间的算法，用2^{O(CC_α(G))}/ε²比特通信达到至少α-ε的效用。(2) 证明了该指数依赖CC_α(G)是紧的（至多差常数因子）。(3) 证明此前工作中的结构假设（信息替代性、弱可学习性）等价于CC_α(G)=O(1)，因此是本文条件更严格的特殊情况。

Conclusion: 本文严格弱化了多智能体信息聚合文献中所需的假设条件，确立了"短协议存在性"这一自然条件足以驱动高效通信。所提出的粗化定理不仅支撑了主算法，还可能在其他通信和博弈论问题中具有独立价值。

Abstract: Our results show that the existence of a short high-utility protocol already suffices for efficient communication. In particular, in a game with $n$ possible observations and $m$ actions: (1) For any achievable target utility $α$, we give an algorithm with $\mathrm{poly}(n, m, 1/ε)$ runtime that designs a protocol achieving utility at least $α-ε$ using only $2^{\mathcal O(CC_α(G))}/ε^2$ bits of communication. Here, $CC_α(G)$ is the minimum number of bits used by any protocol, even a computationally inefficient one, to achieve utility $α$. (2) We prove that this exponential dependence on $CC_α(G)$ is tight up to a constant. That is, unless $\mathrm P=\mathrm{NP}$, no polynomial-time algorithm can in general find optimal protocols using fewer than $2^{CC_α(G) -2}$ bits.
  We note that our results strictly weaken the assumptions required by prior work in the multi-agent information aggregation literature, filling a gap that had remained elusive even for games with constant $CC_α(G)$. In particular, prior guarantees for agreement-based information aggregation rely on structural assumptions such as informational substitutes or weak learnability. We show that these assumptions already imply $CC_α(G) = O(1)$ and are therefore more restrictive conditions than required by our protocol to succeed.
  On a technical level, our results involve a novel strengthening of the Frieze-Kannan weak regularity lemma and yield the following powerful polynomial-time transformation tool: for every communication game $G$, it constructs a game $\hat G$ that is a coarsening of the agents' observation spaces into constant-size partitions, such that $G$ and $\hat G$ are indistinguishable with respect to every short communication protocol. This coarsening theorem is the engine behind our algorithm and may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [347] [A Polynomial-Time Rule Satisfying Full Justified Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05397)
*Fabian Frank,Jannik Peters*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种满足完全正当表示（FJR）的贪婪正当候选规则的变体，可在多项式时间内计算，解决了FJR委员会能否高效计算的问题；同时否定地回答了MES返回的委员会是否总能扩展以满足FJR的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于审批的多赢家投票中，比例代表性是理想目标，但现有规则（如MES）是否总能生成满足FJR的委员会尚不清楚，且FJR委员会的计算效率是一个悬而未决的问题。

Method: 提出一种新的贪婪正当候选规则的变体，该变体在保持FJR满足性的同时可在多项式时间内完成计算。同时对MES委员会的FJR可扩展性进行分析并给出否定性答案。

Result: 新提出的规则变体可在多项式时间内计算并满足FJR，回答了FJR委员会高效计算的开放问题；此外，证明了MES返回的委员会不一定能扩展为满足FJR的委员会。

Conclusion: 通过构造性方法解决了FJR委员会的高效计算问题，并揭示了MES在FJR可扩展性方面的局限性。

Abstract: In approval-based multiwinner voting, voters submit an approval ballot on which basis a committee of fixed size $k$ has to be selected. Compared to single-winner voting this makes it possible to represent minorities in the selection of the committee as well. Thus, one of the most desirable goals of multiwinner voting is to satisfy proportional representation. In this paper we propose a variant of the Greedy Justified Candidate rule that satisfy the notion of Full Justified Representation (FJR). This variant can be computed in polynomial time answering the open question of whether an FJR committee can always be computed efficiently. Additionally, we answer an open question whether committees returned by MES can always be extended in a way to satisfy FJR negatively.

</details>


### [348] [Full Justified Representation under Hare and Droop Quotas in Polynomial Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05417)
*Yizhou Ai*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了审批制多赢家选举中的完全公正代表性（FJR）公理，分别在Hare和Droop两种配额约定下进行。作者提出了一种“递减预算”算法：选民将其剩余预算分配给当前的代表缺口，当累积出价达到共同价格时候选人被“购买”。当候选人价格为λ_H=n/k时，算法返回满足Hare-FJR的委员会；当价格为λ_D=n/(k+1)时，返回满足更强的Droop-FJR公理的委员会（由Casey和Elkind提出）。两种保证共享一个历史支付不变式和终局行列计数论证，而Droop证明在全部k个席位填满时需要新的剩余预算论证。两种变体在选民和候选人顺序固定后均为确定性算法，时间复杂度为O(kmn)次有理数运算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在审批制多赢家选举中，公正代表性（JR）及其加强版本（如EJR、FJR）是衡量委员会公平性的关键公理。Full Justified Representation（FJR）是较新的且更强的公理，但此前关于FJR的算法研究主要在Hare配额约定下展开，Droop配额下的FJR是否可实现尚不清楚。本文旨在为两种配额约定提供统一的确定性算法，填补Droop-FJR构造性研究的空白。

Method: 提出一种递减预算（descending-budget）算法：每个选民拥有初始预算，在迭代过程中将其剩余预算按比例分配给自己有代表性的“缺口”（即尚未满足偏好的候选人），当某一候选人的累积分配达到预设价格λ时被买入。算法设置两种价格：λ_H=n/k对应Hare配额，返回Hare-FJR委员会；λ_D=n/(k+1)对应Droop配额，返回Droop-FJR委员会。证明结构上，两种保证共享历史支付不变式（保证选民不会在已支付候选人上重复投入）和终局行列计数论证；Droop情况还需要额外的剩余预算论证来处理全部k个席位被选满的情形。两变体均为确定性算法，复杂度为O(kmn)次有理运算。

Result: 1. 在Hare配额下，所提算法能够在O(kmn)复杂度内确定性地返回一个满足Hare-FJR的委员会。2. 在Droop配额下，通过调整候选人价格为λ_D=n/(k+1)并引入新的剩余预算论证，算法能够返回满足更强公理Droop-FJR的委员会。3. 两种算法变体共享统一的分析框架（历史支付不变式和行列计数论证），表明两种配额下的FJR可实现性可以在一套递减预算框架内统一处理。

Conclusion: 本文证明了FJR在Hare和Droop两种配额约定下均可通过一个统一的确定性递减预算算法实现。这是首次给出Droop配额下FJR的构造性算法保证，扩展了此前仅限Hare配额的结果。该工作不仅深化了对FJR公理可实现性的理解，也为多赢家选举的设计提供了高效、确定的算法工具。

Abstract: I study Full Justified Representation (FJR) in approval-based multiwinner elections under both the Hare and Droop quota conventions. I introduce a descending-budget algorithm in which voters distribute their remaining budgets across their current representation gaps and candidates are purchased whenever the resulting offers cover a common price. With candidate price $λ_H=n/k$, the algorithm returns a Hare-FJR committee; with candidate price $λ_D=n/(k+1)$, it returns a committee satisfying the more demanding Droop-FJR axiom of Casey and Elkind. The two guarantees share a historical-payment invariant and a terminal row--column accounting argument, while the Droop proof requires a new residual-budget argument when all $k$ paid seats are filled. Both variants are deterministic once the voter and candidate orders are fixed and use $O(kmn)$ rational operations.

</details>


### [349] [AI-Farol: Co-Evolutionary Dynamics in a Multi-Agent Two-Sided Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05479)
*Iosif Polenakis,Kalliopi Kastampolidou,Theodore Andronikos*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文重新构想了El Farol Bar博弈，将酒吧从被动约束转变为具有AI学习能力的战略参与者。通过引入部分可观测性和动态定价机制，将协调问题建模为有限理性代理与适应性机构之间的协同进化过程，为拥堵管理、资源分配和机制设计提供新洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统El Farol Bar博弈将酒吧视为被动的容量约束，忽略了场所本身作为制度设计者的能动性。作者希望突破这一局限，通过赋予酒吧战略决策能力，更真实地反映现实中协调机制中制度层面的自适应调节作用。

Method: 在原始框架上做了两个方向的扩展：(1) 引入部分可观测性，代理只能观察到过往参与者的子集；(2) 将酒吧从被动容量阈值转变为主动机制设计者，通过政策学习优化动态定价，以平衡收入、利用率与可持续性约束。双方分别采用AI学习（代理形成信念并调整参与策略，酒吧优化定价策略），构成双面学习系统。

Result: 该框架将协调问题重塑为有限理性代理与自适应制度之间的协同进化过程，为理解拥堵管理、资源分配及复杂适应系统中的机制设计提供了理论视角。

Conclusion: 通过将单一代理侧学习扩展到双侧重学习（代理与机构），本文展示了如何将经典博弈论模型与现代AI驱动的机制设计相结合，为复杂自适应系统中的协调问题提供了更丰富的建模框架。

Abstract: The El Farol Bar game is a classical model of coordination under uncertainty that traditionally treats the venue as a passive constraint. In this work, we reconceptualize the problem by modeling the bar as a strategic player endowed with AI-driven learning capabilities. We extend the original framework in two principal directions: first, by introducing partial observability, whereby agents observe only subsets of past attendees; and second, by transforming the bar from a passive capacity threshold into an active mechanism designer that adjusts pricing policies to balance revenue, utilization, and sustainability constraints. Agents employ AI-based learning to form beliefs and adapt attendance strategies under incomplete information, while the bar applies policy learning to optimize dynamic pricing. The resulting two-sided learning system frames coordination as a co-evolutionary process between boundedly rational agents and an adaptive institution, offering insights into congestion management, resource allocation, and mechanism design in complex adaptive systems.

</details>


### [350] [Candidate Resignation Monotonicity in Approval-Based Committee Elections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06156)
*Yeeseok Oh,Dominik Peters*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了ABC选举中当选成员辞职的场景，提出"辞职单调性"公理（重新运行投票规则后仍应选出所有剩余的已获胜候选者）。研究发现许多著名ABC规则不满足该性质，且正当代表（JR）公理与辞职单调性不相容。基于最大流思想的分数委员会投票可以同时满足代表公理与辞职单调性；作者设计了整数版最大流规则（最大支付规则MPR），满足放宽形式的辞职单调性和PJR+公理。MPR在一般情形下是NP难的，但在结构化域中可计算。此外还研究了落选者引入弱候选者操纵选举的策略性场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实选举中当选委员可能因各种原因辞职，需要重新运行投票规则补齐席位。现有ABC规则缺乏对辞职后结果稳定性的关注，因此需要一个形式化的公理来刻画"辞职后原获胜者不应被排除"这一合理要求，并考察现有规则的性质。

Method: 形式化定义辞职单调性公理，系统检验现有ABC规则是否满足该公理；通过将其与动态候选集上的ABC选举工作联系起来，证明JR公理与辞职单调性不相容；提出基于网络流表示的最大支付规则（MPR），并分析其计算复杂度（一般情形NP难，结构化域可计算）；最后研究策略性操纵场景。

Result: 证明了众多著名ABC规则不满足辞职单调性；JR公理与辞职单调性不相容；基于最大流的分数委员会投票可同时满足代表公理和辞职单调性；提出的MPR规则满足PJR+和放宽形式的辞职单调性；MPR一般情况NP难计算但在结构化域可解；所有辞职单调规则及许多顺序规则对弱候选者操纵具有免疫性，而PAV规则可被此类策略操纵。

Conclusion: 辞职单调性是ABC选举中一个重要但难以同时满足的公理，与JR等核心代表公理存在根本冲突。不过通过最大流方法可以设计出兼顾代表性与辞职稳定性的规则（MPR），尽管其计算代价在一般情形下较高。这为未来在实际投票系统中采用此类规则提供了理论基础，同时也揭示了PAV等规则在动态和策略性场景下的脆弱性。

Abstract: Approval-based committee (ABC) elections select a fixed-size committee of candidates based on voters' approval preferences. We study a setting where elected members may resign and formalize resignation monotonicity: when we re-run the voting rule after resignations, a resignation monotone rule must still elect all remaining previously winning candidates. We show that many prominent ABC rules fail this property, and by connecting our notion to prior work on ABC elections with dynamic candidate sets, we show that the justified representation (JR) axiom is incompatible with resignation monotonicity. It turns out that fractional committee voting does not suffer from this issue: rules based on using maximum flows in the network representation of an election instance can satisfy both representation axioms and resignation monotonicity. We design an integral version of maximum flow, the Maximum Payment Rule (MPR), and show that it satisfies a relaxed form of resignation monotonicity that only requires there to be some way of replacing the resigning candidates in a way that guarantees the PJR+ axiom. MPR is NP-hard to compute in general, but becomes tractable in structured domains. Finally, we study a strategic setting where a losing candidate may introduce new weak candidates to the election to try to become a winner. We show that all resignation monotone rules as well as many sequential rules are immune, but that the PAV rule can be manipulated in this way.

</details>


### [351] [Fair and Efficient Balanced Allocations for Additive Valuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06325)
*Benjamin Cookson,Nisarg Shah,Paritosh Verma*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究不可分割商品在均衡约束（任意两个代理人的捆绑大小至多相差1）下的公平与效率分配存在性问题。主要结果证明了对于任意可加效用，存在满足EF1（最多嫉妒一个商品）和fPO（分数帕累托最优）的均衡分配，推广了Kawase等人(2026)仅针对个性化双值效用或至多两种估值类型的结果。证明采用KKM引理结合加权福利对偶框架，并发展了一种新颖的价格交错引理以克服先前工作的障碍。该技术进一步扩展到类别约束（划分拟阵约束），在此设定下建立了满足类别敏感放松版EF1的fPO分配存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在公平分配理论中，同时追求公平性（如EF1）和效率（如fPO）是一个核心挑战。额外的均衡约束（捆绑大小差异至多为一）增加了问题的难度。先前工作仅在受限的估值类别（个性化双值或至多两种估值类型）下证明了存在性，本文旨在建立更一般情形下的存在性结果。

Method: 采用Knaster-Kuratowski-Mazurkiewicz (KKM)引理应用于加权福利对偶框架；发展了一个新颖的价格交错引理（price-interlacing lemma）来克服先前工作中遇到的障碍；将该技术进一步扩展到划分拟阵约束（类别约束）场景。

Result: 对于任意可加估值，证明了存在同时满足均衡约束、EF1和fPO的分配；推广了Kawase等人(2026)的结果。在更一般的类别约束（划分拟阵）下，建立了存在满足类别敏感放松版EF1的fPO分配。

Conclusion: 本文通过新颖的证明技术显著推广了均衡约束下公平高效分配的存在性结果，从受限估值类型扩展到任意可加估值，并将方法扩展到更一般的组合约束场景，为该领域提供了更强的理论保证。

Abstract: We study the existence of fair and efficient allocations of indivisible goods under the balancedness constraint, which requires that any two agents' bundles differ in size by at most one. Our main result establishes the existence of balanced allocations that satisfy envy-freeness up to one good (EF1) and fractional Pareto optimality (fPO) for arbitrary additive valuations. This generalizes a recent result of Kawase et al. (2026), which establishes existence only for personalized bivalued valuations or when there are at most two distinct valuation types. Our proof applies the Knaster-Kuratowski-Mazurkiewicz (KKM) lemma to a weighted-welfare duality framework and develops a novel price-interlacing lemma to overcome barriers encountered by prior work. We extend this technique to category constraints, also known as partition-matroid constraints. In this setting, we establish the existence of an fPO allocation satisfying a weaker, category-sensitive relaxation of EF1, under which envy can be eliminated by removing at most one good from each category. All proofs in this paper were obtained using GPT-5.6-Sol with guidance from the authors. The authors verified the proofs, expanded the exposition, and simplified the arguments with assistance from GPT-5.6-Sol and Claude Fable 5.

</details>


### [352] [Resourced Authority A Mechanism-Design Model for Participatory Governance of Deployed AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06353)
*Praphul Chandra,Sujit Gujar,Ganesh Ghalme*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于持续治理已部署AI代理的正式机制设计模型，通过资源分配（计算预算）实现授权自我强制执行，并建立了计算作为有效治理杠杆的"安全AI"范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理被广泛部署，如何持续有效地治理其行为成为一个关键挑战。现有方法缺乏正式、自我执行的治理机制。作者提出以计算预算作为治理杠杆，通过资源分配实现授权的自我强制，从而保证治理决策能被实际执行而不依赖外部执法。

Method: 构建了一个治理周期为扩展形式博弈的机制：经认证的人类利益相关者按顺序到达并在供给/拒绝市场上用治理货币投票；资金聚合器将原始贡献转化为广度加权的有效支持；双阈值门控（带滞回）将净支持转换为二元授权；通过受外生成认证的安全上限约束的耦合映射，释放计量计算预算——以有符号计算许可证形式在硬件中实现，使决策自我执行。

Result: 刻画了该机制能够治理的代理类别；将被治理代理操纵治理选民的操控问题确定为核心开放问题，并提出了若干相关挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作将治理建模为部署者之上的合规/公共资源覆盖层，证明了计算预算是可行的治理杠杆，但被治理代理对选民的操控是未来需要解决的关键难题。

Abstract: We give a formal mechanism design model for the continuous participatory governance of a deployed AI agent. The mechanism is built on the principle that governance should control an AI agent through resource allocation so as to make authorization self enforcing via compute budgets. The mechanism seeks to establish the Safe AI paradigm that compute is an effective governance lever. We situate our work as a compliance or commons overlay on a deployer. One governance period is an extensive form game in which verified human stakeholders arrive sequentially and contribute, on a provision or a rejection market, in a governance currency that is deliberately distinct from the agents compute. A funding aggregator turns raw contributions into breadth weighted effective supports - a two threshold gate with hysteresis converts net support into a binary authorization that, through a coupling map bounded by an exogenously certified safety ceiling, releases a metered compute budget - realized in hardware as a signed compute license so that the decision is self-enforcing. We characterize the class of agents the mechanism can govern and isolate manipulation of the governing electorate by the governed agent as the central open problem. We also introduce several challenges addressing manipulation of governing electorate by the governed agents.

</details>


### [353] [AV-AIVAT: 74x Cheaper Agent Evaluation with Certified Anytime-Valid Stopping in Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06362)
*Boning Li,Yu Chen,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出AV-AIVAT方法，将AIVAT方差缩减技术与可随时有效的置信序列（Confidence Sequences）结合，使不完全信息博弈中的agent强度评估能在证据充分时及时停止，同时保持名义置信水平不失效。在HUNL场景中，AIVAT将方差中位数减少54倍；结合AsympCS后，原始结果需要中位数74倍手数才能达到同等停止精度；EB-CS提供精确有限样本认证，HUNL中停止时间比中位数为1.37倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在评估两个agent谁更强时，需要玩博弈直到技能超过运气的影响，但每局游戏都消耗金钱、推理算力或专家时间。由于所需对局数未知，固定预算评估要么在结论已定时继续浪费资源，要么在可区分前过早停止；而朴素的可选停止（optional stopping）搭配普通置信区间会破坏声明的置信水平。因此急需一种既能及时停止又能保持统计保证的评估方法。

Method: 本文提出AV-AIVAT（Anytime-Valid AIVAT），将AIVAT方差缩减方法与持续监测的置信序列（Confidence Sequences）相结合。核心设计包括：1) 在线值模型仅从历史对局中学习，使得当前对局不会被自身修正所影响；2) 使用Asymptotic CS（AsympCS）进行渐近筛查，实现快速早期停止；3) 使用Empirical-Bernstein CS（EB-CS）进行精确有限样本认证，其中被修正收益的上界需要独立证明；4) 在Leduc hold'em中结构性地建立该界，并刻画CS下注上限与界定决定的方法宽度下限，从而量化方差收益转化为更早停止的程度。

Result: 在71,439手配对HUNL对局中，跨15个LLM agent配置，AIVAT将方差中位数减少54倍。在名义95%置信水平和±1 Big Blind目标精度下，原始结果需要中位数74倍手数才能达到AIVAT修正结果的停止条件（使用AsympCS）。描述性HUNL EB-CS运行显示停止时间比中位数为1.37倍。AV-AIVAT成功将方差缩减转化为高效的早期停止。

Conclusion: AV-AIVAT将方差缩减转化为高效、可审计的早期停止，同时将渐近筛选与精确认证分离，使评估能在证据充分的时刻停止，并为第三方提供在停止时刻复查结论所需的全部信息，从而保证了评估的统计有效性与资源效率的兼顾。

Abstract: Deciding which of two agents is stronger means playing games until skill outweighs luck, and every game costs money, model inference, or expert time. Since the number of games needed is unknown, fixed-budget evaluations either keep paying after the result is settled or stop before the agents can be told apart, while naive optional stopping with an ordinary confidence interval invalidates the stated level. We make such an evaluation stop as soon as its evidence suffices, with the guarantee intact. The Action-Informed Value Assessment Tool (AIVAT) reduces variance in imperfect-information games through conditional mean-zero corrections, by a median $54\times$ across 15 LLM agent configurations spanning 71,439 paired Heads-Up No-Limit Hold'em (HUNL) hands, but does not say when to stop. We combine AIVAT with continuously monitored Confidence Sequences (CSs) into anytime-valid AIVAT (AV-AIVAT), whose online value model learns only from past games so that no game scores its own correction. At the nominal 95\% level and a target precision of $\pm1$ Big Blind, raw outcomes need a median $74\times$ as many hands as AIVAT-corrected outcomes to stop under the Asymptotic CS (AsympCS). Exact finite-sample certification uses the Empirical-Bernstein CS (EB-CS), which needs an independently justified bound on corrected payoffs. We establish such a bound structurally for Leduc hold'em and characterize a width floor set by the CS's bet cap and that bound, which governs how much of a variance gain becomes earlier stopping; the descriptive HUNL EB-CS runs show a median $1.37\times$ stopping-time ratio. AV-AIVAT turns variance reduction into efficient, auditable early stopping while separating asymptotic screening from exact certification, so an evaluation can stop the moment its evidence suffices and hand a third party everything needed to recheck the verdict at that very stopping time.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [354] [Media Meets Communication in 6G: Fundamentals, Key Technologies, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05184)
*Bingyan Xie,Longyu Zhou,Zihan Chen,Shunpu Tang,Mingyang Shi,Yu Tian,Guo Lu,Yongpeng Wu,Tianhao Liang,Tony Q. S. Quek,Guangtao Zhai,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了面向6G视觉通信的媒体通信技术，提出了一个包含AI驱动媒体技术、媒体感知无线传输、大模型媒体通信和智能网络基础设施四个关键维度的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络加速媒体智能与通信智能的融合，媒体通信正在从传统比特级传输向智能、语义感知和生成式范式演进。新兴媒体服务不仅需要高数据率和低延迟，还需要语义感知、感知质量保障、自适应资源配置、可信内容处理和个性化媒体生成，因此亟需系统化的综述来梳理这一领域的发展。

Method: 通过回顾通信与媒体技术的演进历程，阐明媒体内容处理与无线传输之间的内在关系，提出一个由四个关键维度构成的统一框架：(1) AI驱动媒体技术（媒体编码、内容理解、质量评估、安全合规检测、AIGC媒体生成）；(2) 媒体感知无线传输（语义联合信源信道优化、信源感知传输优化、信道感知信源优化三个互补视角）；(3) 大模型赋能的媒体通信；(4) 智能网络基础设施。

Result: 提出了面向6G视觉通信的媒体通信技术系统性综述，建立了包含四个关键维度的统一框架，并从语义联合信源信道优化、信源感知传输优化和信道感知信源优化三个互补视角系统分析了媒体感知无线传输技术。

Conclusion: 该调查为6G时代媒体通信技术的发展提供了系统化的研究框架，涵盖了从AI驱动媒体技术到智能网络基础设施的完整技术栈，为未来研究和实践指明了方向。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of sixth-generation (6G) networks is accelerating the convergence of media intelligence and communication intelligence, driving media communication beyond conventional bit-level delivery toward intelligent, semantic-aware, and generative paradigms. Emerging media services require not only high data rates and low latency, but also semantic awareness, perceptual quality assurance, adaptive resource orchestration, trustworthy content processing, and personalized media generation. Meanwhile, media technologies are evolving from handcrafted signal processing and conventional coding toward artificial intelligence (AI)-driven representation learning, content understanding, and generative reconstruction. Motivated by these trends, this paper presents a systematic survey of media communication technologies for 6G vision communication by revisiting the evolution of communication and media technologies and clarifying the intrinsic relationship between media content processing and wireless transmission. We introduce a unified framework consisting of four key dimensions: AI-driven media technologies, media-aware wireless transmission, large model-enabled media communication, and intelligent network infrastructures. Specifically, AI-driven media technologies encompass media coding, content understanding, quality assessment, security and compliance detection, and AIGC-enabled media generation, while media-aware wireless transmission is examined from three complementary perspectives: semantic joint source-channel optimization, which jointly encodes task-relevant semantic information; source-aware transmission optimization, which leverages media characteristics for channel adaptation, prediction, and compensation; and channel-aware source optimization, which adapts media coding and reconstruction based on real-time channel conditions.

</details>


### [355] [Diversity in Coded TE-QKD Channels: Achieving Infinite Diversity out of Finite System Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05432)
*Shaikha S. Al-Qahtani,Siyao Li,Joseph J. Boutros*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间纠缠量子密钥分发（TE-QKD）信息协调中纠错码的多样性阶问题。令人惊讶的发现是：在信道具有有限多样性且码长相对较短的情况下，译码器竟然能实现无限多样性阶——这在经典编码与通信理论文献中从未出现过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TE-QKD信息协调中的多样性阶问题此前未被研究过。经典衰落信道中，译码只能将有限多样性阶乘以有限因子，而本文发现TE-QKD场景下可以实现无限多样性，这一结果颠覆了传统认知，需要通过严格的条件和证明来揭示其本质。

Method: 对于有界距离代数译码，本文导出了以每码字光子数和译码半径为变量的充要条件；对于软判决译码，引入了最大有限多样性（MFD）性质，并证明当且仅当码是MFD缺陷的时才能实现无限多样性。最后用基于Golay码、RS码、BCH码和Reed-Muller码的短码实例进行了验证。

Result: 首次证明TE-QKD信息协调中译码器可以实现无限多样性阶，尽管信道多样性有限且码长相对较短。该结果在经典衰落信道中没有对应物，并通过多种短码的仿真验证了分析的正确性。

Conclusion: TE-QKD中无限多样性的实现条件已被完全刻画：有界距离代数译码取决于光子数和译码半径，软判决译码取决于码是否满足MFD缺陷。这揭示了量子通信中一种全新的编码增益机制，为TE-QKD系统设计与短码选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We establish conditions and give proofs on how an error-correcting code can attain infinite diversity in a time-entanglement quantum key distribution (TE-QKD) reconciliation. The shocking result, never encountered in the literature on coding and communication theory, is that a decoder exhibits an infinite diversity order while the channel has finite diversity and the code has a relatively short finite length.
  This paper studies the diversity order of coded TE-QKD reconciliation, defined by the asymptotic slope of the error probability at high signal-to-noise ratio. For bounded-distance algebraic decoding, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition in terms of the number of photons per codeword and the decoding radius.
  For soft-decision decoding, we introduce the maximal finite diversity (MFD) property and prove that infinite diversity is achieved if and only if the code is MFD deficient. The infinite diversity in TE-QKD has no counterpart in classical fading channels, where decoding can only multiply a finite diversity order by a finite factor.
  Examples of short codes based on Golay, Reed-Solomon, Bose-Chaudhuri-Hocquenghem (BCH), and Reed-Muller codes validate the analysis and illustrate how the TE-QKD system parameters and the relatively short code parameters affect the achievable diversity for both hard and soft information reconciliation.

</details>


### [356] [An Ordered-Reliability-Bits Chase Decoding Algorithm for BCH Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05591)
*Wenwu Zhu,Min Zhu,Baoming Bai*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于BCH码的低复杂度有序可靠度比特Chase（ORB-Chase）译码算法。该算法与传统Chase算法有两个关键区别：一是采用逻辑权重作为度量来生成测试错误模式（TEPs），二是引入了基于整数的早停准则，能在识别出最大似然码字时尽早停止计算。仿真结果表明，该算法能以显著更少的测试模式实现接近最大似然的性能，且平均BM译码调用次数随信噪比增加而快速下降，相比Chase算法最多可减少98.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Chase译码算法在BCH码译码时需要生成大量测试错误模式并执行多次BM译码，计算复杂度较高。作者希望设计一种低复杂度的译码算法，在保持接近最大似然译码性能的同时，显著减少测试模式数量和计算开销，从而提高译码效率。

Method: 提出的ORB-Chase算法包含两个核心改进：（1）采用逻辑权重作为度量来生成测试错误模式，不同于传统Chase算法中基于可靠度排序的方式；（2）引入基于整数的早停准则，当识别出最大似然码字时能在最早可能阶段停止计算。算法通过有序可靠度比特的选择策略替代穷举式的TEP生成，并将早停条件转化为整数比较以降低实现复杂度。

Result: 针对(127,113,5) BCH码和(256,239,6) eBCH码的仿真结果表明：ORB-Chase算法在显著减少测试模式数量的前提下实现了接近最大似然的性能；平均BM译码调用次数随Eb/N0增大而快速下降，在相同BLER性能下相比Chase算法最多可减少98.1%的BM译码调用。

Conclusion: ORB-Chase算法在保持接近最大似然译码性能的同时，通过逻辑权重生成TEP和整数早停准则有效降低了计算复杂度，是一种适用于BCH码的实用低复杂度译码方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a low-complexity ordered-reliability-bits Chase (ORB-Chase) decoding algorithm for BCH codes. The proposed algorithm differs from the traditional Chase algorithm in two key aspects. First, it employs the logical weight as a metric to generate test error patterns (TEPs). Second, it introduces an integer-based early termination criterion that ensures computation can stop at the earliest possible stage if the maximum-likelihood codeword is identified, thereby minimizing unnecessary computational effort. Simulation results for (127, 113, 5) BCH codes and (256, 239, 6) eBCH codes demonstrate that the ORB-Chase algorithm achieves near-ML performance with significantly fewer test patterns compared to the Chase algorithm. Moreover, the average number of Berlekamp-Massey (BM) decoding calls decreases rapidly as $E_b / N_0$ increases, achieving a reduction of up to 98.1% compared to the Chase algorithm at the same BLER performance.

</details>


### [357] [Maximal achievable service rates of some classes of linear codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05657)
*Priyanka Choudhary,Monika Yadav,Maheshanand Bhaintwal*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了几类线性码（包括循环码和LDPC码）中数据符号最大可达服务速率的下界，这些码源自组合结构如t-设计、差集和平衡不完全区组设计（BIBD）。作者建立了系统线性码和非系统二进制码中每个数据符号最大可达服务速率的下界，并利用BIBD关联矩阵构造的码及其设计参数来确定这些下界。此外，还分析了系统扩展线性码，证明了对称BIBD的存在可推导出相应系统循环码服务速率的下界，并给出了基于差集构造的若干循环码族的显式下界，特别确定了由Singer差集产生的循环码每个数据符号最大可达服务速率的精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性码中数据符号的最大可达服务速率（maximum achievable service rate）下界这一核心问题，特别是利用组合结构（t-设计、差集、BIBD）来推导这些下界，从而更好地理解和设计具有高服务速率的线性码。

Method: 1) 针对系统线性码（假设C^⊥中固定重量码字支撑形成BIBD）和非系统二进制码（假设C^⊥中固定重量码字支撑形成t-设计）分别建立每个数据符号最大可达服务速率的下界；2) 利用BIBD关联矩阵构造的线性码（特别是BIBD-LDPC码），利用底层设计的参数确定服务速率下界；3) 分析系统扩展线性码的服务速率；4) 利用对称BIBD与对偶码字之间的对应关系推导系统循环码服务速率下界；5) 从差集构造循环码族并给出显式下界，特别针对Singer差集确定精确值。

Result: 建立了系统线性码和非系统二进制码数据符号最大可达服务速率的通用下界；展示了BIBD设计参数如何用于推导BIBD-LDPC码的服务速率下界；证明了SBIBD对应关系可推导系统循环码的服务速率下界；给出了基于差集的循环码族服务速率下界；对Singer差集的循环码，确定了每个数据符号最大可达服务速率的精确值。

Conclusion: 组合设计理论为推导线性码数据符号最大可达服务速率的下界提供了强有力的工具。通过BIBD、t-设计和差集等组合结构，不仅能够获得统一的下界框架，还能对某些特定构造（如Singer差集相关循环码）给出精确值，为线性码在分布式存储等场景中的性能分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate lower bounds on the maximum achievable service rates for data symbols in certain classes of linear codes, including cyclic codes and low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes, that are derived from combinatorial structures such as $t$-designs, difference sets, and balanced incomplete block designs (BIBDs). We first establish a lower bound on the maximum achievable service rate for each data symbol in the following two cases: (i) systematic linear codes $C$ under the assumption that the supports of codewords of a fixed weight in $C^\perp$ form a BIBD, and (ii) non-systematic binary codes under the assumption that the supports of codewords of a fixed weight in $C^\perp$ form a $t$-design. We then investigate the linear codes obtained from the incidence matrices of BIBDs, particularly certain classes of BIBD-LDPC codes, and show how the parameters of the underlying designs can be exploited to determine lower bounds on the maximum achievable service rates for the data symbols of the corresponding linear code. In addition, we analyze the maximum achievable service rates of systematic extended linear codes. We show that the existence of a symmetric BIBD (SBIBD) corresponding to a dual codeword can be used to derive a lower bound on the maximum achievable service rate of the associated systematic cyclic code. We also present some families of cyclic codes constructed from difference sets and obtain explicit lower bounds on the maximum achievable service rates for their data symbols. In particular, we determine the exact values of the maximum achievable service rates for each data symbol of cyclic codes arising from Singer difference sets.

</details>


### [358] [Wireless Linear Computation Broadcast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05692)
*Shuo Tan,Syed A. Jafar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了无线线性计算广播（WLCBC）框架，将经典的线性计算广播问题扩展到高斯MIMO广播信道，并针对噪声接收端侧信息场景设计了联合线性收发机优化算法，通过交替优化（LMMSE解码器与凸QCQP预编码器更新）实现加权和MSE的单调收敛，仿真验证了显著的鲁棒性增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作仅探索了无噪声理想广播信道下的LCBC信息论容量，但实际无线场景中存在噪声、功率约束和异构侧信息，需要将LCBC理论扩展到更真实的物理层约束下，以应对带有噪声侧信息的MIMO广播信道中的高效多用户线性函数计算需求。

Method: 将高斯MIMO广播信道中的WLCBC问题形式化为加权和MSE最小化问题，采用交替优化：固定预编码器时，最优解码器为LMMSE估计器；固定解码器时，预编码器更新转化为凸QCQP问题，通过单对偶变量参数化获得半闭式解，保证目标函数单调递减并收敛。

Result: 所提算法保证WSMSE目标函数的单调递减与收敛，且仿真实验表明相较于从先前工作自然衍生出的基线方案，该方案在多种配置下展现出显著的鲁棒性增益。

Conclusion: 成功地将LCBC从理想无噪BC推广至带噪声侧信息的Gaussian MIMO BC，建立了高效且收敛有保证的集中式联合线性收发机设计算法，为无线场景下的线性函数计算广播提供了可行的框架。

Abstract: A linear computation broadcast (LCBC) problem comprises $K$ users (receivers) and a transmitter. The users wish to compute various (vector) linear functions of a common dataset, and possess in advance heterogeneous side information corresponding to various other linear computations. The goal for the broadcast transmitter, who knows the dataset and all desired and side-information functions, is to satisfy all demands as efficiently as possible. Prior work has explored the information-theoretic capacity of LCBC for zero-error finite-field computation over an ideal (noiseless) broadcast channel (BC). This paper develops a wireless LCBC (WLCBC) framework for the Gaussian MIMO BC with noisy receiver-side information under a transmit power constraint and arbitrary antenna configurations. In the WLCBC setting, a Gaussian source is linearly precoded for broadcast; each user requests a linear function of the source and forms an estimate by linearly combining its channel observation with its noisy side information. Assuming perfect channel state information, we cast the centralized joint linear transceiver design as a weighted sum-MSE minimization problem and propose an efficient alternating optimization algorithm. For a fixed precoder, the optimal decoders are the linear minimum mean-square error (LMMSE) estimators. For fixed decoders, the precoder update reduces to a convex quadratically constrained quadratic program which leads to a semi-closed-form solution parameterized by a single dual variable. The resulting algorithm guarantees a monotonic decrease in the objective and convergence of the weighted sum-MSE (WSMSE) objective sequence. Simulations demonstrate pronounced robustness gains over natural baselines obtained from prior works.

</details>


### [359] [Moment-based linear programming bounds for locally recoverable codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05758)
*Shujian Li,Hengjia Wei,Maosheng Xiong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文为q元(r,δ)-局部可恢复码(LRCs)推导了新的Delsarte型线性规划界，具有三个特征：变量集规模与经典Delsarte LP相当；利用局部距离条件强制的δ-2阶高阶信息，其平衡基部分与非退化线性码的对称化精化权重LP给出相同的维数界，且附加约束在δ≥3时非平凡并提供进一步强化；适用于一般(线性及非线性)(r,δ)-LRCs。在二元和三元字母表上的大量计算表明，凸包LP能提供先前LP无法获得的改进，并常常优化缩短界和广义Singleton界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的(r,δ)-局部可恢复码的界（如缩短界、广义Singleton界及先前的LP界）在刻画这类码的维数上仍有改进空间，需要更强且适用于线性和非线性码的统一上界工具。

Method: 作者推导了新的Delsarte型线性规划界，其变量集规模与经典Delsarte LP相当；该LP利用局部距离条件强迫产生的阶δ-2高阶信息，平衡基部分与非退化线性码的对称化精化权重LP（Gruica-Jany-Ravagnani）给出的维数界一致，而附加约束在δ≥3时非平凡，进一步强化了界。该方法通过凸包LP实现，适用于一般(r,δ)-LRCs（线性和非线性）。

Result: 在二元和三元字母表上的大量计算显示，所提出的凸包LP界优于先前LP方法能捕捉到的界，并且常常能改进缩短界和广义Singleton界。

Conclusion: 新的Delsarte型LP为(r,δ)-局部可恢复码提供了更紧且广泛适用的上界，是先前方法的推广和强化。

Abstract: In this paper we derive new Delsarte-type linear programming bounds for $q$-ary $(r,δ)$-locally recoverable codes (LRCs) with three attributes: first, the variable set is comparable in size to that of the classical Delsarte LP; second, our LP exploits the higher-order information forced by the local-distance condition through order \(δ-2\), in the sense that for nondegenerate linear codes, its balanced base part gives exactly the same dimension bound as the symmetrized refined-weight LP of Gruica, Jany, and Ravagnani, while the additional constraints, nonvacuous whenever $δ\ge 3$, give a further strengthening; and third, it applies to general $(r,δ)$-LRCs, linear and nonlinear alike. Extensive computations over binary and ternary alphabets show that the convex-hull LP yields improvements not captured by the previous LP and often sharpens the shortening and generalized Singleton bounds.

</details>


### [360] [Chernoff-Stein-Type Exponent in Testing Between Two Outlier Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05933)
*Ligong Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个随机离群值检测问题：在$2^{nR}$条长度为$n$的随机序列中，有一条序列是离群值，其索引随机。在$\mathcal{H}_0$下离群值服从$Q_0$分布，在$\mathcal{H}_1$下服从$Q_1$分布，其余序列在两种假设下均服从$P$分布。在$\mathcal{H}_0$下错误概率有界远离1的约束下，推导了$\mathcal{H}_1$下错误概率的最快指数衰减速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题具有广泛的应用背景，例如在通信、雷达、异常检测等场景中，需要从大量候选序列中检测出唯一的异常源。其数学本质是在指数规模的随机序列集合中，区分由离群值引起的两种假设，属于多元假设检验与离群值检测的交叉问题，具有重要的理论意义。

Method: 采用指数速率（误判指数）分析方法。将问题建模为在$2^{nR}$条序列中识别出唯一离群值并对两种假设进行判定的假设检验问题，在Neyman-Pearson型约束（$\mathcal{H}_0$错误概率有界远离1）下，研究$\mathcal{H}_1$错误概率的指数衰减速率，并对该速率进行精确刻画。

Result: 在$\mathcal{H}_0$下错误概率被限制为有界远离1的条件下，得到了$\mathcal{H}_1$下错误概率最大的指数衰减率，即最优误判指数，给出了该指数的闭式表达式。

Conclusion: 本文为大规模随机序列集合中的离群值检测问题提供了精确的指数级性能界限，揭示了$Q_0$、$Q_1$与$P$之间的分布关系以及序列数指数$R$对检测性能的影响。

Abstract: Among $2^{nR}$ length-$n$ random sequences, one sequence is an outlier whose index is random. Under hypothesis $\mathcal{H}_0$, the components of the outlier are independent and identically distributed (IID) according to $Q_0$, whereas under hypothesis $\mathcal{H}_1$ they are IID according to $Q_1$. The remaining $(2^{nR}-1)$ sequences are mutually independent, independent of the outlier, and IID according to $P$ under both hypotheses. Based on the observation of all $2^{nR}$ sequences, one wishes to decide between $\mathcal{H}_0$ and $\mathcal{H}_1$. Under the constraint that the decision error probability under $\mathcal{H}_0$ must be bounded away from $1$, we determine the fastest exponential decay rate of the decision error probability under $\mathcal{H}_1$.

</details>


### [361] [A Session Interaction Framework for The Multiple-Unicast Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06042)
*Sirui Liu,Zongpeng Li,Xiying Fan,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个"会话交互框架"（Session Interaction Framework），将无向网络中的多播猜想（multiple-unicast conjecture）转化为一个核心等价命题：该猜想在全体网络拓扑上成立当且仅当每个不可约核（irreducible core）是独立的。框架通过两阶段流程（归约与分解）来系统性验证该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多播猜想是网络编码领域的关键开放问题，其成立与否不仅决定网络编码在无向网络中是否优于路由，还蕴含计算复杂性理论中的根本性下界。因此，探索一个能将全局可行性问题化归为可处理子问题的系统性框架具有重要意义。

Method: 提出会话交互框架，包含两个关键机制：(1) 归约阶段——提供会话支配（session dominance）的简化充分条件，以几何判据迭代简化复杂会话集合；(2) 分解阶段——提出会话解耦定理（Session Decoupling Theorem），将会话集合的猜想有效性归结为其独立子集；此外还证明了被高代价割或割点分隔的会话必然独立。

Result: 将多播猜想的全局可行性问题转化为两阶段过程：先通过几何判据进行可操作的归约，再对不可约核利用解耦定理进行分解验证。建立了"猜想普遍成立 ⟺ 每个不可约核独立"的等价关系，并给出拓扑条件下会话独立的充分保证。

Conclusion: 该框架为在一般网络拓扑上验证多播猜想提供了系统化方法论，将复杂全局问题分解为局部可检验的子问题，为最终攻克该猜想奠定了结构化的理论工具。

Abstract: The multiple-unicast conjecture asserts that network coding offers no throughput advantage over routing in undirected networks. Its validity is known to imply fundamental lower bounds in computational complexity. We propose a Session Interaction Framework that reduces the conjecture to a central equivalence: the conjecture holds universally if and only if every irreducible core is independent. This result transforms the global feasibility problem into a two-stage process. First, to make the reduction phase tractable, we provide simplified sufficient conditions for session dominance, offering geometric criteria to iteratively simplify complex session sets. Second, for the remaining "irreducible core," we propose a Session Decoupling Theorem, reducing the conjecture's validity for a session set to its independent subsets. Topologically, we prove that sessions separated by high-cost cuts or cut-vertices are guaranteed to be independent. By integrating these reduction and decomposition mechanisms, our framework offers a systematic methodology to verify the conjecture across general network topologies.

</details>


### [362] [On the Multiple-Unicast Conjecture: Beyond Cut Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06070)
*Sirui Liu,Li Que,Zongpeng Li,Baochun Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对网络编码中著名的"多单播猜想"（multiple-unicast conjecture），从图度量（graph metrics）的视角出发，建立了一个统一的度量框架来研究编码与路由之间的吞吐量比较，并基于此框架证明了该猜想在多个新网络类别中成立，包括至多五个终端位置的网络、满足特定面边界条件的平面网络、以及具有特定结构限制的一般网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多单播猜想是网络编码领域超过二十年的核心开放问题，断言在任何无向网络中，编码相对于分数路由对独立单播会话没有吞吐量优势。该猜想与计算复杂性理论深度关联（若能证明将给出基础问题的长期寻求的下界），但目前缺乏统一的数学工具来系统研究它。本文旨在通过将基本对象从"割"（cuts）扩展到"图度量"（graph metrics），建立新视角来攻克这一难题。

Method: 本文建立了一个统一的度量框架，将网络编码与路由比较背后的基本对象从割提升为图度量。基于该框架，作者针对三类新的无向网络类别证明了猜想成立：包括至多五个终端位置的网络、终端位置分布在至多三个指定面边界上且每对会话端点位于同一个面上的平面网络，以及具有特定结构限制（涉及会话端点）的任意规模网络。此外，作者还给出了一种无需计算机辅助搜索的新证明，证明猜想在至多六个编码节点的网络中成立。

Result: （1）证明了多单播猜想对至多五个终端位置的网络成立；（2）证明了猜想对终端位置位于至多三个指定面边界的平面网络成立（每会话端点在同一面上）；（3）证明了猜想对满足特定会话端点结构限制的任意规模网络成立；（4）给出了无计算机辅助搜索的新证明，表明猜想对至多六个编码节点的网络成立；（5）证明了若猜想在Γ₃,₃网络上成立，则所有不含三个会话具有六个不同终端位置的网络也成立。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入图度量视角的统一框架，为多单播猜想提供了新的研究工具，并将该猜想的已知成立类别显著扩展，涵盖了新的网络类别并提供了更简洁的证明方法。这为最终解决这一开放问题奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Network coding allows intermediate nodes to encode received messages before transmission. The multiple-unicast conjecture asserts that coding has no throughput advantage over fractional routing for independent unicast sessions in any undirected network. Despite more than two decades of sustained study, this central open problem remains unresolved. The conjecture is deeply connected to computational complexity: a proof would yield long-sought lower bounds for fundamental problems. To study the conjecture, this paper develops a unified metric framework from the perspective that the basic objects behind the comparison between coding and routing are not cuts alone, but graph metrics. Using this framework, we prove the conjecture for three new classes of undirected networks: (a) networks with at most five terminal locations; (b) planar networks whose terminal locations lie on the boundaries of at most three designated faces, with each session's endpoints on one such face; and (c) networks with arbitrarily many nodes and terminal locations under a structural restriction on session endpoints. We give a new proof that the conjecture holds for networks with at most six coding nodes, without computer-aided search, and show that if the conjecture holds on $Γ_{3,3}$, then it holds whenever no three sessions have six distinct terminal locations.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [363] [GenGA: Editable and Data-Grounded Graphical Abstract Generation for Academic Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05478)
*Takuro Kawada,Shunsuke Kitada,Hitoshi Iyatomi*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出GenGA框架，将图形摘要(Graphical Abstract)生成从光栅图像转变为可编辑的矢量图形，并引入结构独立系数(SIC)量化图形的编辑简便性，实验证明GenGA在编辑简便性、简洁性和语义一致性上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图形摘要自动生成方法输出光栅图形，难以进行后编辑（如修改文字、调整布局），无法满足论文写作和同行评审中迭代修改的需求。

Method: 1) 定义"从论文内容生成可编辑图形摘要"的新任务；2) 提出GenGA框架，以层级结构的矢量元素集合形式直接生成图形；3) 引入结构独立系数(SIC)度量局部修改对其他元素的传播程度，量化图形的编辑简便性。

Result: GenGA在编辑简便性上优于传统方法，在简洁性和语义一致性上甚至超越人类撰写的图形摘要；SIC被验证为与人工编辑成本相关的有效度量指标。

Conclusion: 该研究将图形摘要生成重新定义为可编辑矢量图形生成问题，契合研究者的实际工作流程，显著促进有效的科学传播。

Abstract: Graphical Abstracts (GAs) visually summarize the key findings of academic papers, playing a crucial role in facilitating the understanding of research content. Recently, advancements in vision-language models and image generation models have enabled the automatic generation of scientific figures based on paper content. However, most conventional methods output the generated results as raster graphics, making post-editing (e.g., text modification and layout changes) highly difficult. This poses a significant challenge, as they are unsuitable for the iterative figure revision process inherent in paper writing and peer review. To tackle these challenges, we define the novel task of generating editable GAs from paper content and propose GenGA, a new GA generation framework that directly produces figures in vector format. By generating figures as a collection of vector elements with a hierarchical structure, GenGA produces outputs that can be seamlessly imported into existing drawing tools for intuitive, element-level editing. Furthermore, we introduce the Structural Independence Coefficient (SIC), a metric that quantifies the editing simplicity of a figure based on the degree to which local modifications propagate to other elements. Experimental results show that GenGA achieves superior editing simplicity compared to conventional methods, and even surpasses human-authored GAs in conciseness and semantic alignment. We also validate SIC as an effective metric correlated with manual editing costs. This study fundamentally redefines GA generation as an editable vector graphic generation problem grounded in the practical workflows of researchers, significantly promoting effective scientific communication.

</details>


### [364] [ESVR: 3D Ellipsoid-based Sparse Volume Rendering via Structure-aware Primitive Learning and Per-primitive Ray Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05564)
*Suemin Jeon,Youjin Kim,Jungwoo Park,Kyungryun Lee,Won-Ki Jeong*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: ESVR是一种基于椭球体的稀疏体渲染框架，直接从原始体数据中学习紧凑的椭球体基元，实现大规模稀疏体数据的高效压缩与实时渲染，相比现有3DGS方法避免信息丢失并支持灵活的传递函数交互控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模稀疏体数据的高效表示与渲染仍是科学可视化的挑战。直接体渲染（DVR）质量高但计算和存储成本随数据规模增长而恶化。现有3DGS方法从DVR渲染图像而非原始体数据学习，导致信息丢失，且限制了灵活传递函数控制在交互探索中的应用。

Method: ESVR框架包含四个关键技术：1）带有限支撑的可微椭球体基元；2）结构感知的基元学习与互补剪枝策略；3）每基元光线采样策略以实现快速精确的传递函数映射；4）支持大规模数据的基于块的优化方案，引入幽灵椭球体在训练时提供边界上下文。

Result: 在多个大规模稀疏数据集上，ESVR实现了高达四个数量级的压缩比，渲染帧率达43-223 FPS，同时保持有竞争力的重建质量。

Conclusion: ESVR通过直接在3D空间学习椭球体基元，克服了现有3DGS方法从图像学习导致的信息损失问题，实现了大规模稀疏体数据的高效实时渲染与灵活交互探索，展示了基于基元的稀疏体渲染方法的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Efficient representation and rendering of large-scale sparse volumetric data remain challenging in scientific visualization, as meaningful structures often occupy only a small fraction of the spatial domain. While direct volume rendering (DVR) provides high-quality visualization, its computational and memory costs scale poorly with data size. Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) address this challenge by representing volumetric scenes with compact geometric primitives, enabling efficient, high-fidelity rendering. However, existing 3DGS-based methods learn from DVR rendered images rather than raw volumes, leading to information loss and limiting flexible transfer function control for interactive exploration. To address these limitations, we propose ESVR, an ellipsoid-based sparse volume rendering framework that directly learns and renders volumetric data in 3D space. Our method combines differentiable ellipsoidal primitives with bounded support, structure-aware primitive learning with complementary pruning, and a per-primitive ray sampling strategy for fast and accurate transfer function mapping. To support large-scale datasets, we further introduce a chunk-based optimization scheme with ghost ellipsoids, providing boundary context during training. Across large sparse datasets, ESVR achieves up to four orders of magnitude compression and real-time rendering at 43-223 FPS while maintaining competitive reconstruction quality.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [365] [Discrete energy as an exact label-free training objective for finite-element surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05437)
*Ruifeng Cao,Xidan Song*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文记录并证明了线性弹性静力学中有限元代理模型可进行无标签训练的数学恒等式：组装后的离散势能与参考解能量之差等于刚度范数误差平方的一半，能量梯度等于刚度加权误差，因此无标签离散能量最小化与有监督的刚度范数回归具有相同唯一极小值和相同梯度。文中还包含若干辅助定理（位移误差界、模方向收缩恒等式、CG后处理的切比雪夫界、JEPA预训练的条件性潜在分离命题），并用可执行的反事实检验验证了所有数值声明。最后说明了该构造无法直接推广到弹性动力学的原因及恢复精确性的时间离散化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练有限元代理模型通常需要参考解，而每个参考解本身需要求解代理模型试图替代的系统，形成循环依赖。本文旨在消除这一瓶颈，通过利用组装后的离散势能作为无需参考解的监督信号，实现无标签训练。

Method: 核心方法是通过数学证明建立关键恒等式：预测能量与参考解能量之差等于1/2的刚度范数平方误差，能量梯度等于刚度加权误差。由此证明无标签离散能量最小化与有监督刚度范数回归等价（相同唯一极小值和相同梯度）。此外辅以若干命题：位移误差的能量间隙界、模方向收缩恒等式（解释欧氏位移误差为何不适合作为主度量）、CG后处理的切比雪夫界、JEPA在共享刚度算子上的条件性潜在分离。每个数值声明均实现为可执行验证检查。

Result: 所有不等式的可执行验证检查均在两组测试上通过：合成测试问题集和来自预注册实验验证集的16个实例探针集，且报告了度量的紧度。数值反例限定了JEPA条件性潜在分离命题的适用范围。弹性动力学部分的结论是直接最小化作用泛函不成立，需特定的时间离散化格式才能恢复精确性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了线性弹性静力学中无标签的离散能量最小化训练信号与有监督的刚度范数回归在数学上完全等价，为有限元代理模型的免参考解训练提供了严格的理论基础。该方法不能直接推广到弹性动力学，但存在能恢复精确性的时间离散化格式，为后续动态问题的无标签训练指明了方向。

Abstract: Supervised training of finite-element (FE) surrogate models requires reference solutions, and each reference solution is obtained by solving the system that the surrogate is intended to replace. The assembled discrete potential energy provides a training signal that requires no reference solution. This note records, with proofs, the identities that make this signal exact for linear elastostatics: the difference between the energy of a prediction and the energy of the reference solution equals one half of the squared stiffness-norm error, and the gradient of the energy equals the stiffness-weighted error. Label-free discrete-energy minimisation and supervised regression in the stiffness norm therefore have the same unique minimiser and identical gradients at every point. Around this central result, the note states a conditioning lemma that bounds the displacement error by the energy gap, a modewise contraction identity that explains why the Euclidean displacement error is an unsuitable primary metric, the Chebyshev bound that governs conjugate-gradient post-processing of surrogate predictions, and a conditional latent-separation proposition for joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) pretraining on a shared stiffness operator, with an explicit numerical counterexample that delimits its scope. Every claim with numeric content is implemented as an executable falsification check; the checks were executed twice, on synthetic test problems and on a probe set of 16 instances from the validation split of a pre-registered experimental run, and every inequality holds, with the measured tightness reported. A closing section explains why the construction does not extend to elastodynamics through direct minimisation of the action functional, and which time-discrete formulation restores exactness.

</details>


### [366] [Variable-Horizon Workforce Demand Forecasting with an Aggregate Demand Constraint for Construction Workforce Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05551)
*Hanbyeol park,Jaehyeon Heo,Taekhyun Park,Minseong Kim,Hyerim Bae*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出约束保持残差分配预测（CP-RAF）方法，用于解决施工项目中劳动力需求预测面临的可变预测周期和总量约束问题。该方法将观察到的劳动力需求时间序列表示为系数向量，检索形状相似的历史已完成任务，通过相似度加权平均估计剩余任务期间的分配概况，并据此分配预先确定的剩余劳动力需求，从而在保持总量约束的同时适应可变预测周期。现场数据评估显示，CP-RAF在中长期固定长度预测中优于八个基线模型，且在可变长度预测下保持较低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施工项目中劳动力规划是反复出现的运营决策，需要准确预测各任务的未来劳动力需求。然而实践中存在两个核心挑战：一是不同任务的完工日期不同导致预测周期可变；二是预测的每日劳动力需求总和必须等于预先指定的总劳动力配置量。现有基于机器学习的预测模型大多假设固定长度输出，且未显式施加总量需求约束，无法满足这些运营要求。

Method: CP-RAF方法包含三个关键步骤：首先将观察到的劳动力需求时间序列表示为系数向量；其次检索具有相似时间形状的已完成任务；然后通过相似性加权平均估计剩余任务持续时间的分配概况。预先定义的每项任务剩余劳动力需求按照估计的分配概况进行分配，预测周期在保持概况特征的同时进行调整。该流程同时容纳了可变预测周期并保持了总量需求约束。

Result: 使用劳动力需求现场数据进行评估。结果表明，CP-RAF在中长期固定长度预测中优于八个基线模型，并在可变长度预测条件下维持了较低的预测误差。

Conclusion: 通过将运营约束直接纳入预测流程，CP-RAF为建筑施工中的劳动力配置提供了适用框架，解决了现有ML预测模型在可变预测周期和总量约束方面的不足。

Abstract: Workforce planning is a recurring operational decision during construction projects that requires accurate forecasts of the future workforce demand for individual tasks. However, in practice, tasks have different completion dates, resulting in variable forecast horizons. In addition, the sum of the predicted daily workforce demands must equal the total workforce allocation specified in advance. Most existing machine-learning (ML)-based forecasting models assume fixed-length outputs and do not explicitly impose an aggregate demand constraint, making them unsuitable for these operational requirements. To address this problem, this study proposes constraint-preserving residual allocation forecasting (CP-RAF). The CP-RAF represents an observed workforce demand time series as a coefficient vector and retrieves completed tasks with similar temporal shapes. Then, it estimates the allocation profile over the remaining task duration using similarity-weight averaging. The predefined remaining workforce demand for each task was distributed according to the estimated profile, and the forecast horizon was adjusted while retaining profile characteristics. This procedure accommodates variable forecast horizons while preserving the aggregate demand constraints. CP-RAF was evaluated using workforce demand field data. The results showed that CP-RAF outperformed eight baseline models in medium- and long-horizon fixed-length forecasting and maintained low forecast errors under variable-length forecasting. By directly incorporating operational constraints into the forecasting procedure, the proposed method provides a framework suitable for workforce allocation in construction practices.

</details>


### [367] [A Label-Free Physics-to-Data Acceleration Framework for Parametric Time-Dependent PDEs with Latent-Space Differential-Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05554)
*Hongjiang Wang,Weizhe Wang,Yingzheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: PHD-SF是一个紧凑的物理到数据谱系框架，通过SD-TPD分离策略和微分算子学习，在统一DON架构下集成PIDON、HIDON和DIDON三种运行模式，实现无标签的时间相关参数化偏微分方程高效加速求解，仅需一个或两个代表性参数案例的初始物理信息阶段即可实现跨参数推理，端到端成本低于训练单个常规PINN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的参数化PDE求解器分为物理信息求解器和数据驱动代理模型两大类，前者物理一致性强但计算成本高，后者推理高效但依赖高保真数据集。这两类方法孤立发展，通常仅通过显式解场标签连接，缺乏统一的框架来同时利用两者优势并减少对大规模高保真数据生成的依赖。

Method: 提出PHD-SF框架，核心技术包括：(1) SD-TPD分离策略，将空间特征、潜在动态特征和空间微分算子作为可重用模型信息；(2) 统一DON（深度算子网络）架构下定义三种运行模式——PIDON（物理信息模式）、HIDON（超约简增强模式）和DIDON（直接推理模式）；(3) 模型信息可在三种模式间生成、继承和丰富，实现无标签谱系建模，无需预计算全场解标签或显式标签迁移；(4) 建立分离求解、超约简式增强和直接推理的加速路径。

Result: 在四个基准时间相关参数化PDE上，仅使用初始物理信息阶段的1-2个代表性参数案例即可实现准确的跨参数求解和直接推理。总端到端成本低于训练单个常规PINN的成本，同时支持可重用的跨参数推理。

Conclusion: PHD-SF为多查询参数化PDE提供了一条高效的求解路径，通过统一的物理到数据谱系框架显著降低了对大规模高保真数据生成的依赖及其离线成本，在推理效率和物理一致性之间取得了有效平衡。

Abstract: Efficient solution of time-dependent parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) is central to computational science and engineering. Existing deep-learning-based accelerators span a physics-to-data spectrum, from physics-informed solvers with strong physical consistency but high computational cost to data-driven surrogates with efficient inference but strong dependence on high-fidelity datasets. These methods have largely evolved in isolation and are often connected only through explicit solution-field labels. We propose PHD-SF, a compact physics-to-data spectrum framework for accelerating time-dependent parametric PDEs.
